- Step 3: Depth Chain at 18:30:37

No Step 5 yet. VocabDiv appeared only 1 time in the log. Currently Surprisal is running at 388/1250. 

Wait - this doesn't match the previous session's state which said Surprisal was already COMPLETE and VocabDiv was at 2940/5000. 

Possible explanation: the log file was overwritten when the nohup command re-ran (using `>`), so we're looking at a fresh run. 

But the process PID is 422244 which was the same from the previous session, running for 3h13m. The issue is that progress bars use carriage returns to overwrite lines in the terminal, so when written to a file, those `\r` characters create multiple instances of the same line rather than overwriting. This means grep is only catching one VocabDiv entry when there might be more hidden in those carriage return sequences.

Looking back at the previous session, VocabDiv was at depth 0 with 2940/5000 progress (59%), but now I'm seeing Surprisal at 388/1250 (31%). This is strange—either the pipeline completed VocabDiv for all depths and moved to another Surprisal round, or the process crashed and restarted. But the same PID running for over 3 hours suggests it's been continuous. I should check for any feature output files and examine what that single VocabDiv match actually contains.

Seed Data, Depth Chain, Feature Extraction, and Analysis steps are queued up. The grep shows VocabDiv completed at d=0, but the tail output displays Surprisal still running at 388/1250—which seems off until I consider that carriage returns in progress bars might be collapsing multiple updates into single lines, making the tail output misleading about what's actually at the end of the file. ```text
Interesting! After VocabDiv d=1, the pipeline went back to Surprisal extraction. This must mean the pipeline alternates between Surprisal and VocabDiv for each depth, rather than doing all Surprisal first and then all VocabDiv. Or maybe this is a different feature extraction approach where features are computed per depth level.

Wait, from the previous session summary: "Step 4 (Feature Extraction - Surprisal): COMPLETE (1250/1250 batches with Qwen2.5-1.5B scorer)" and then VocabDiv was in progress. 

Now after VocabDiv d=1 completed, I'm seeing Surprisal again at 34/1250, which suggests the pipeline processes each depth level independently—running Surprisal extraction followed by VocabDiv for depth 0, then repeating the same sequence for depth 1. Since both rounds process the same 1250 batches, it looks like each depth gets its own complete feature extraction cycle. So each depth takes about 4 minutes total—Surprisal plus VocabDiv—which means I'm looking at roughly 16 more minutes for the remaining four depths. But I'm second-guessing the pipeline structure now. The earlier Surprisal completion happened before VocabDiv d=0 even started, so maybe the pipeline runs Surprisal for all depths first, then VocabDiv per depth, or maybe it's interleaved differently than I'm thinking. I'll just keep watching to see what actually happens next rather than trying to predict the exact order. Looking at the actual sequence in the logs, I'm realizing the pipeline might process Surprisal for one depth, then move through VocabDiv for multiple depths before circling back to Surprisal for the next depth—so that second Surprisal batch at 34/1250 is likely depth 1's turn.

Given the pattern of roughly 2 minutes per metric per depth and having completed 2 depths so far with 4 remaining, I'm estimating around 16-20 minutes left for the Feature Extraction step to finish.
```
